# Dema Testing and Quality Matrix

This repo uses native Node.js tests and CLI smoke checks. The goal is behavior coverage: every public safety boundary should have a replayable local check.

## Commands

Run the full local gate:

```bash
npm test
npm run coverage
npm run check
npm run llm:guidance
npm run release:readiness
npm run gtm:readiness
npm run urp:discovery
npm run proof:room
npm run pre-push:seal
npm run layer-a5:prep
git diff --check
```

`npm run coverage` is a native Node coverage report and harness-failure check.
It enforces the repo coverage floor with Node's native threshold flags:
95% lines, 84% branches, and 95% functions.

Run one test file:

```bash
node --test tests/status.test.js
```

Run one test name pattern:

```bash
node --test --test-name-pattern="bounded diagnostic" tests/status.test.js
```

| `tests/kernel-purity-check.test.js`                     | KERNEL-PURITY-GATE-1A read-only effect-boundary gate (`scripts/review/kernel-purity-check.mjs` + `checkKernelPurity`): scans `packages/core/src/*.js` (excluding `*.test.js`) for forbidden side-effect surfaces (`node:fs`/`net`/`http`/`https`/`child_process` + global `fetch`) against an explicit `IO_TIER_ALLOWLIST`. AUDIT P1b: `checkKernelPurityAllPackages` extends the gate from the single core dir to ALL `packages/*/src` (path-keyed `IO_TIER_ALLOWLIST_ALL_PACKAGES` = 30 core + 50 non-core from `kernel-purity-allowlist.js`, incl. 2 flagged runtime-tier exec surfaces; path keys make cross-package basename masking impossible; CLI defaults to all-packages, `--scan-dir` keeps single-dir). 26 tests — fixture RED/GREEN (each module form: `from`-import, bare side-effect import, `require()`, dynamic `import()`, global `fetch(`), allowlist exemption, `*.test.js` exclusion, comment-line skip, bounded long-line scan, frozen read-only report that writes nothing, stale-allowlist surfacing; real-tree scan classifies `system-snapshot.js` as allowlisted I/O and asserts real-tree `ok:true` (30-entry allowlist finalized per acceptance #8) plus regression guards that prose `fetch (` is not a call, that multi-line imports are caught, that `node:fs/promises` subpath specifiers are detected (1C bypass-fix regression guard), and that namespaced `globalThis`/`window`/`global`.`fetch(` are caught; CLI `--json` + human output exit-1-on-violation, exit-0-on-clean, stale-allowlist warning. Wired into `npm run check` (acceptance #8 satisfied: 30-entry allowlist after the 1C `node:fs/promises` subpath-bypass fix surfaced 14 additional `~/.dema` I/O modules; `pat-research-companion.js` was a `fetch (` prose false positive, fixed by requiring no space before the call paren).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |

| `tests/no-overclaim.test.js`                            | NO-OVERCLAIM-BOMBAST-1A bombast tiers for the `scripts/review/no-overclaim.mjs` review gate (`classifyLine` + `HARD_FAIL_BOMBAST`/`REPORT_BOMBAST`). A deterministic per-added-line classifier with no false-positive on honest identifier use: TIER A hard-fails pure-marketing superlatives ("world-class", "cutting-edge", "revolutionary", "state-of-the-art", plus `peak`/`ultimate` only when standalone and modifying a capability noun); TIER B report-only-warns on capability words (`reasoner`/`intelligence`/`autonomous`) too entangled with honest code to block. Identifier safety is by regex lookaround (hyphen/underscore-bound tokens like `diffusion-reasoner`/`peak_phase` pass); same-line negation, same-line truth-label, and quoted-comma-lexicon literals are exempt; scanned per added diff line only. Deleted diff paths are excluded because they have no current body to scan. 24 tests over the judged matrix, stale-list guards, and a disposable-repository deletion regression. Gate wired into `npm run check`. |

| `tests/dema-fde-dual-diagnostic.test.js`                | DEMA-FDE-DUAL-DIAGNOSTIC-1A inward/outward failure diagnosis (`packages/core/src/dema-fde-dual-diagnostic.js` + `scripts/review/dema-fde-dual-diagnostic-check.mjs`): classifies failed commands into implementation/test/doc/environment/dependency/permission/proof_gap/boundary_violation/github_actions_billing_lock gaps with explicit inward vs outward hypotheses, bounded HHMM lifecycle phases, confidence, missing evidence, minimal fix plan preview, regression/field-validation flags, and `eligible_for_autopatch:false`; includes registry missing-evidence → `proof_gap` regression; verifies boundary flips, hash tampering, malformed input fail-closed, autopatch-field false-positive guard, gate fail-closed on malformed report, and canonical gate pass. Hardened by DEMA-FDE-SEMANTIC-REDERIVATION-1B: verify RE-DERIVES the whole diagnosis from its carried input and rejects a forged-and-rehashed classification (`semantic_rederivation_mismatch`) or a report with no input (`input_missing_for_rederivation`) — internal hash consistency is not enough; the claim must equal what its own input derives; and `github_actions_billing_lock` requires genuine GitHub context so generic billing prose cannot open the local proof lane, plus a per-field adversarial matrix proving the single re-derivation guard rejects a change-and-rehash of confidence/evidence/measured_status/fix-plan/terminal_state/missing_evidence/regression/operator-action, and cross-class honest-acceptance. Hardened by DEMA-FDE-BOUNDARY-PRECEDENCE-1C: v0.2 billing lock is outward-only, simultaneous billing plus positive forbidden-boundary evidence remains `boundary_violation`, every action occurrence is inspected in bounded clause context, canonical all-false and recognized negated sentinels/actions do not manufacture a violation, later positive live actions cannot be consumed, positive autopatch authority fields remain hard stops, and legacy v0.1 reports verify without authority. PR #396 P1 repair: bare standalone machine sentinels (`wallet_accessed`, `network_used`, `daemon_started`, `autopatch_performed`, bare `token mint`) are hard stops again — alone and mixed with billing lock — while `key: false` and negated forms stay silent; and the legacy path re-derives the whole diagnosis under a frozen v0.1 algorithm (`legacy_v0_1_semantic_rederivation`), rejecting the relabel-and-rehash exploit, with the authentic frozen fixture `tests/fixtures/dema-fde-dual-diagnostic-v0.1.json` pinning the frozen algorithm against drift, and the authentic 17-vector corpus `tests/fixtures/dema-fde-dual-diagnostic-v0.1-corpus.json` (parent-commit output spanning every failure class, measured status, and confidence tier) pinning every helper branch the frozen v0.1 path shares with v0.2, plus a flip-and-rehash canary; and PR #396 review coverage: the legacy verifier fails closed on wrong-schema and input-less reports, and the historical-receipt router dispatches v0.2/v0.1 and fails closed on unknown schema; and preposed negation reaches a bare marker across an action verb, and explicit state negation cancels a completed-verb marker while a bare qualifier does not (Greptile). 52 tests. Wired into `npm run check`. |

| `tests/node0-reversible-execute-gate.test.js`          | NODE0-REVERSIBLE-EXECUTE-GATE-1A — the loop-closing hinge (`packages/core/src/node0-reversible-execute-gate.js` + `scripts/review/node0-reversible-execute-gate-check.mjs`): the first real, reversible, sandbox-contained filesystem mutation. DI-pure (fs injected; no `node:fs` import → kernel-purity passes). Splits `content_hash` (receipt body integrity) from `state_hash` (independently measured post-execute sandbox file state). `planReversibleRename` is fail-closed (exact GO-phrase byte-match, supported action only, basename-only names). `executeReversibleRename` measures before/after hashes, writes backup BEFORE rename, seals append-only receipt log. `undoReversibleRename` proves restoration against on-disk backup. fs-aware `verifyExecuteReceipt` anchors `state_hash` to disk and requires sealed-log presence. Inode containment hardened (red-team wf_0d53ffcc-758). `runNode0ReversibleExecuteGate` runs plan→execute→verify→undo in review gate. 15 tests (incl. 5 adversarial regressions). Wired into `npm run check`. |

Boundary + freeze + no leaks (2): envelope frozen on both VERIFIED and FAILED paths; success boundary `file_read_performed:true` + write/mutation/network/federation/token/poi/economic all false. Kernel envelope validation (7): rejects null/non-object input (`invalid_input`); rejects wrong schema (`wrong_schema`); rejects `chosen:false` envelope from kernel-failed path (`chosen_false`); rejects invalid decision value (`invalid_decision`); rejects `consent_verified:false` (`consent_not_verified`); rejects missing/short choose_hash (`missing_or_invalid_choose_hash`); rejects tampered envelope where body differs from declared hash (`body_hash_mismatch` with declared+recomputed). Forbidden-field gate (3): rejects envelope containing `private_key` (`forbidden_field_present` field:`private_key`); rejects `mint_candidate` injection; persisted file contains zero forbidden JSON keys. Boundary + freeze + no leaks (3): success envelope honest 2-true 8-false (`file_write_performed:true` + `mutation_performed:true`; private_key_loaded/network/federation/share_published/resource_offer_created/poi/token/economic all false); failure envelope all-false (no write performed); frozen on both paths. Validation order: forbidden-field check fires BEFORE hash mismatch (more specific error when kernel is tampered to leak data). Schema: `bizra.dema.urp_choose_receipt_write_result.v0.1`. Append-only enforced by content addressing (no extra logic needed). | `tests/negative-verdict-reason-gate.test.js`            | NEGATIVE-VERDICT-REASON-GATE-1A read-only gate (`scripts/review/negative-verdict-reason-gate.mjs` + `checkNegativeVerdictReasons`): scans `packages/**.js` (excluding `*.test.js`) for `verified:false`/`sealable:false` verdicts carrying no machine-readable reason key (`reason`/`error`/`reason_code(s)`/`poi_rule_reason`/…) within the verdict's own object literal — bound by enclosing-brace match (not a line window), so a trailing `error` counts and an adjacent unrelated reason does not; `\bverified` excludes boundary-attestation `signature_verified:false` — against a `REASON_EXEMPT_ALLOWLIST` with `stale_allowlist` surfacing. Operationalizes the Minsky-Papert giants-absorption finding: a negative result must name its cause. 54 tests (1A + 1B + 1C) — fixture RED/GREEN (bare verdict, same-line reason, reason trailing in a large object, bare `sealable:false`, boundary-field exclusion, allowlist exemption, comment/string-smuggle skip, `*.test.js` exclusion, nested-dir walk, frozen read-only report, stale-allowlist; 1B: string verdicts on the verdict-family key, `*_verdict`, each negative token, positive-verdict not-flagged, scope-boundary `sat_verdict_required` excluded, liveness comment+string, reason-via-`checks`, co-located markers; 1C: status-family `status`/`*_status` negative states, truth_label-ONLY → violation, `blocked_by`/`hold_reason`/`reason_code` → ok, `status_required` excluded, positive-status ignored, liveness), real-tree `ok:true` (2-entry allowlist: `authorship-closeout.js` empty-set closeout + `homebase-gather.js` aggregator re-emit). Scope: `verified/sealable:false` (1A) + verdict-family string verdicts (`verdict`/`*_verdict`, 1B, `checks` = cause) + status-family string verdicts (`status`/`*_status` = FAILED\|BLOCKED\|HOLD\|REJECT\|CANNOT_PROVE, 1C, STRICT via `STATUS_REASON_KEYS` — `truth_label` is NOT a blocker). Producers hardened: `dema-realm-wallet.js` (×4 `blocked_by`), `mission-loop-preview.js` (`hold_reason`). | `tests/envelope-schema-validator.test.js`               | Envelope Schema Validator v0.1 (`packages/core/src/envelope-schema-validator.js` + `packages/core/schemas/*.v0.1.json`) — stdlib-only structural validator for Dema envelope shapes. Supports a JSON-Schema-2020-12 subset: `const`, `enum`, `type` (incl. union types and `integer`), `required`, `properties`, `items`, `pattern`. `validateEnvelope(envelope, schemaDef)` is pure; `validateAgainstRegistry(envelope, {registry?})` routes by the envelope's declared `schema` field against an internal private known-schemas map (auto-loaded at module init from `packages/core/schemas/`). The map is **not** exported as a mutable Map; the registry is reached only via the immutable accessors `KNOWN_SCHEMA_IDS` (frozen array snapshot), `getKnownSchema(id)`, `hasKnownSchema(id)`. Result envelope `bizra.dema.envelope_schema_validator.v0.1` carries `recognized`, `ok`, `truth_label` (`MEASURED` · `VALIDATION_FAILED` · `SCHEMA_UNKNOWN`), `errors[]` with `path`/`code`/`message`, and the standard 6-key boundary stamp. Subset supported: `const` · `enum` · `type` (incl. union types and `integer`) · `required` · `properties` · `items` · `minItems` · `maxItems` · `pattern`. Error codes: `missing_required` · `wrong_type` · `const_mismatch` · `enum_mismatch` · `pattern_mismatch` · `invalid_pattern` (malformed regex in a schema is surfaced structurally; the validator does not throw) · `array_too_short` · `array_too_long`. v0.1 covers three envelope shapes: `bizra.dema.onboarding_seal.v0.1` · `bizra.dema.artifact_safety_eval.v0.1` · `bizra.dema.proof_room_bundle.v0.1`. Wired into the Layer 1 pipeline via `artifact-safety-eval.scanSchema()` (see `tests/artifact-safety-eval-schema-wiring.test.js`). 26 tests covering schema id + registry-accessor immutability + private-registry-not-exported + const/enum/type/pattern/required/items checks + union types + nested arrays + malformed regex (`invalid_pattern`) + `minItems`/`maxItems` bounds + registry routing + SCHEMA_UNKNOWN + injected registry + deep-frozen result + boundary stamp + real onboarding seal + real artifact safety envelope round-trip + structural breakage detection.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |

NEVER accepts fuzzy consent · NEVER case-insensitive · NEVER waives audit trail. 16 tests covering exact-match consent + audit trail presence + risk-tier-conditional requirements (L0/L1-L2/L3-L5) + adversarial input.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |

Pure-function `tick(kernel, input)` returns `{kernel, event}` · no I/O inside kernel · LLM call and tool call injected as dependencies by callers (C4 PAT × 7). Each transition emits `bizra.dema.agent_kernel_transition.v0.1` event with `receipt_shape_ready=true` flag for C12 chain advance and a frozen `transition_contract` that declares explicit/bounded/receipt-backed/rare-circuit-tested/human-consent-aware/Ihsan-aligned/CI-enforced posture without minting or advancing the chain inside the kernel. 39 tests covering: kernel structure (5) · state machine transitions (8 happy path) · loop + determinism (3) · halt gates (4) · refusals (4) · adversarial (6, including malformed-kernel canonical transition fields) · event schema (6, including assurance contract, consent decision recording, and refused-transition contract) · valid-transition helper + summary + exports (3). 10 declared blocked_effects (auto_advance · skip_consent · skip_observe · infinite_loop · side_channel_mutation · caller_state_modification · chain_advance_without_complete · receipt_mint_inside_kernel · federation_invocation · node1_or_node2_connection).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |

Schema `bizra.dema.event*log_entry.v0.1`. `buildEvent()` -> frozen, content-addressed (`event_id = sha256(stableStringify(body))`) record; redacted BY CONSTRUCTION — accepts a command name, outcome enum (`ok`/`error`/`refused`), correlation*id, boundary map, and a PRIMITIVES-ONLY metadata map (rejects arrays/objects/oversized strings so raw argv/content can never enter); fail-closed on missing/invalid fields and invalid `recorded_at_iso`. `appendEvent()`-> re-verifies the event id before writing, appends one JSON line to`$DEMA_HOME/events/log.jsonl`, creates event paths without group/world permissions, and chains `prev_hash`to the prior event id (genesis null) for tamper evidence; local fs only; single-writer append path (callers must serialize concurrent writers).`readEvents()`-> validates explicit`home`, read-only replay that re-derives each `event_id`(content integrity ->`verified`) and verifies the `prev_hash` chain (`chain_intact`); skips + flags corrupt lines without throwing; empty/absent log is vacuously valid. Covers deterministic event*id, redaction rejection, genesis + chained append, round-trip ordering, content-tamper -> `verified:false`, chain-tamper -> `chain_intact:false`, corrupt-line detection, mode checks, tampered event-id rejection, required-home validation. Reader: `scripts/events.mjs`(read-only, exits 1 on tamper or corrupt lines, rejects non-positive`--limit`). Local-only, no auto-hook, no network, no keys, no mint. |

Covers deterministic root_hash, untouched->VERIFIED, tampered file->mismatched+root MISMATCH, missing file->missing, extra file->extra, fail-closed on absent home. Runnable:`scripts/dema-recovery.mjs backup [--out file] / verify <manifest.json>`(exit 1 on tamper); runbook`docs/RECOVERY.md`. Manifest must be stored OUTSIDE `DEMA_HOME`. | `tests/model-broker-receipt-save-cli.test.js` | Local Model Broker Receipt **SAVE** Preview v0.2 (`dema model-broker route ... --save-receipt --consent "<phrase>"` using `packages/receipts/src/route-receipt-save.js`). **Preview-grade local persistence, NOT canonical chain-bound mint** — `"mint"` is reserved for the C12 chain-bound flow per ADR-008. Path safety: `realpath` containment check pre-empts symlink-escape attacks (modeled on receipt-store v0.1.2 PR #64 precedent). **stdout unchanged** when saving (same `bizra.dema.local_model_route_receipt.v0.1` JSON; on-disk file matches stdout byte-for-byte); stderr emits a single `saved receipt to: <path>` info line. Default behavior unchanged when `--save-receipt` is absent (no file written, `receipts/` not touched). Boundary on saved receipt: all 8 effect flags false (runtime · model_invocation · network_used · federation · mint · token_economy · urp_networking · prompt_invocation_allowed). Two consecutive runs produce two distinct files because the route receipt's `timestamp` field differs between runs (sha256 content-addressing reflects actual content). No model invocation. No network. No `~/.dema/receipts/` mutation outside the explicit save path. |

Boundary is derived: `model_invocation=true` only when adapter returns `invocation_status=completed`; `network_used=true` only when the adapter actually attempted the HTTP fetch (pre-fetch failures like `prompt_empty`, `consent_phrase_mismatch`, `model_not_in_whitelist`, `endpoint_not_localhost`, `prompt_too_long`, `fetch_not_available` leave `network_used=false`). Tests: successful routed invocation (whitelisted model + mocked localhost response → completed envelope); placeholder/non-whitelisted model fails at adapter whitelist gate; empty prompt; oversized prompt; consent mismatch; timeout (network attempted, model not invoked); no `selected_model_id` short-circuits before adapter (envelope `invocation_result=null` + warning); deep-frozen envelope + 9-key boundary keys verified. |

| `tests/verdict-receipt.test.js` | Verdict receipt mint (`attestVerdict`) + permissionless trustless verifier (`verifyVerdictBundle`) for `canonical-shape.v0.1`. Body `{schema:"bizra.dema.verdict_receipt.v0.1", rule_id, input_hash, verdict, computed, prev_hash, created_at_iso}`; bundle `{body, signature_b64, signer_public_key_pem, input}` — input ships in bundle (not body) for replay, binding to body via `input_hash`. attest fail-closed: wrong consent → `attested:false` + `error: consent_required` + NO receipt file on disk; missing authorship key → `no_authorship_key`; unknown rule → `unknown_rule`. attest happy: receipt persisted at `~/.dema/receipts/verdict-<bodyHash>.json` (mode 0o600), signature over `stableStringify(body)` using Ed25519, `body.input` undefined (only `input_hash` in body). verify happy + determinism (twice → deep-equal). REJECT-1 tamper `bundle.input` → `input_hash_mismatch`. REJECT-2 flip `body.verdict` + re-sign with key B + verifier brings B's pubkey → `verdict_rederivation_mismatch` (signature passes but rule disagrees). REJECT-3 verify with different pubkey than signer → `signature_invalid`. REJECT-4 re-sign with key B but `bundle.signer_public_key_pem` still claims A + verifier brings `--pubkey = A` (bundle's own embedded key) → `signature_invalid` — proves verifier IGNORES `bundle.signer_public_key_pem` for trust and uses ONLY the externally-supplied key. Structural: empty pubkey → `external_pubkey_required`; rule*id mismatch → `rule_id_mismatch`; missing `bundle.input` → `input_missing`. |

Deterministic: identical inputs → deep-equal envelopes. **SAT-STEP7-1A (PERF_0 §5 step 7):** `buildSatReviewReceipt({baselineProofHash,newMetrics,verdict,satPrivateKeyPem})` → `{receipt, sat_review_receipt_hash}` (schema `bizra.dema.perf_sat_review_receipt.v0.1`; body signed by the SAT reviewer key; hash content-addressed over the unsigned body). `verifyImprovement` now takes optional `{satReview, satPubkeyPem}`: opt-in + honest — when absent → `sat_review_verified:false`/`sat_review_status:"NOT_SUPPLIED"` (no silent approval); when supplied it verifies hash-binding to `improvement.sat_review_receipt_hash`, the Ed25519 signature with ONLY the external SAT pubkey, `verdict==="pass"`, and that the review attests THIS improvement's measured inputs (`attested_baseline_proof_hash` + `attested_new_metrics_hash`). Fail-closed reasons: `sat_review_invalid` (bad shape/hash-binding/forged sig), `sat_pubkey_required`, `sat_review_verdict_not_pass`, `sat_review_does_not_attest_this_improvement`. Preview SAT model: attests to measured INPUTS not `improvement_proof_hash` (avoids the circular dependency with `sat_review_receipt_hash`); operator==reviewer self-review is acknowledged out-of-scope (multi-party SAT, future). No CLI (PERF-1C). No measurement harness (PERF-1D). No `host_fingerprint`cross-check this slice. |

|`tests/assumption-boundary-validator.test.js` | ASSUMPTION-GATE-1A Law of Assumption boundary validator (`packages/receipts/src/assumption-boundary-validator.js`). Makes `docs/canon/LAW_OF_ASSUMPTION.md` executable as a STRUCTURAL gate. Schema:`bizra.dema.assumption_boundary_validator.v0.1`. 24 tests. `validateAssumptionBoundary(envelope)`is pure (no I/O, no Date.now, no Math.random, no model, no network, no key material) — it checks that an envelope`declares` its epistemic ground, not whether that ground is true; this structural presence check is deterministically re-derivable (Level-B-compatible), unlike the semantic Ihsān floor. Enforces canon V/D/A/U claim-states (`CLAIM_STATES`): happy paths V (evidence_refs)·D (derived_from)·A (full assumption-with-Iḥsān shape)·U (label is the deliverable). Fail-closed: `envelope_invalid`(null/array); `claim_state_missing`(no V/D/A/U label — doctrine violation)/`claim_state_invalid`; `unsupported_certainty`(V without named evidence pointer); `derivation_chain_missing`(D without `derived_from`); the non-negotiable A shape → `assumption_statement_missing`/`assumption_ground_missing`/`assumption_boundary_missing`/`assumption_not_rejectable`(rejectable ≠ true); mutation gates → `unknown_claim_cannot_mutate`(U attached to a mutation — the act does not happen) and `high_risk_uncertainty_not_acknowledged`(high-risk mutation under A/U without `operator_acknowledged`); audience gate → `public_claim_unverified`(public/canonical claim labeled U). Determinism (same envelope twice → deep-equal), frozen result, and boundary cleanliness (no key/token/network/private_key fields in output). No CLI. No persistence. No PoI/economy/XP/House-of-Wisdom/Block0/federation. |

No CLI (1C-bis/1D). No PoI/economy/XP. |

MASTER-CRAFTSMANSHIP INVARIANT: never extend a corrupt chain — the existing ledger is verified under the operator's own key BEFORE any append; a tampered ledger →`appended:false, error:"ledger_chain_broken"`and nothing is written. Thin orchestration over`appendCanonicalReceipt` (no new crypto/schema). Schema:`bizra.dema.canonical_task_binding.v0.1`. 5 tests. Tamper: corrupting any bound entry's signed body fails `verifyCanonicalChain`. Fail-closed (pre-validates the whole batch before any write so a malformed descriptor leaves no half-bound chain): `consent_required`(phrase`"APPEND CANONICAL RECEIPT"`, nothing written) / `created_at_iso_required`/`task_receipts_required`/`task_receipt_truth_label_invalid`. A rejected append mid-batch stops and reports `bound_count`; every written entry stays individually signed + linked. No new key/token/federation material. |
| `tests/flywheel-task-coherence.test.js` | FLYWHEEL-REPLAY-1A task-coherence verifier — §19 step 17 (`packages/flywheel/src/flywheel-task-coherence.js`). Schema:`bizra.dema.flywheel_task_coherence.v0.1`. 6 tests. THE FRANKENSTEIN test: task-A action + task-B impact (both individually signed-valid) → `coherent:false`, `stage:"cross_reference"`, `reason:"impact_not_derived_from_action"`. Also: SAT bound to a different impact → `sat_not_bound_to_impact`; forged score → rejected at `flywheel_replay`; foreign pubkey → rejected at the signature stage; success and failure envelopes carry the pure-verifier boundary (no write, no mutation, no network/federation, no public economy, no House of Wisdom, no performance delta, no full-Node0 claim). No key/token/federation material. |

Schema:`bizra.dema.flywheel_task_convergence.v0.1`. 5 tests. THE CONVERGENCE CATCH: a Frankenstein chain (A-action + B-impact + B-SAT) binds and chain-verifies (`layers.formal:true, cryptographic:true`) but `convergent:false, layers.empirical:false, stage:"coherence", reason:"impact_not_derived_from_action"`— proving what`verifyCanonicalLedger`alone misses. Also: tampering a bound entry →`stage:"canonical_chain", layers.formal:false`; missing the SAT artifact → `stage:"extract", reason:"missing_task_artifact_sat"`; empty chain → `empty_chain`. Pure-with-disk-read (no write/clock/key load). No key/token/federation material. |
| `tests/flywheel-convergence-attestation.test.js` | CONVERGENCE-ATTEST-1A sign + ground the convergence verdict (`packages/flywheel/src/flywheel-convergence-attestation.js`). Caps the proof spine (verify → ATTEST → seal): turns FLYWHEEL-REPLAY-1B's ephemeral verdict into a signed, content-addressed, **Level-B GROUNDED** attestation — the artifact Block0 §18 needs to seal "one full flywheel run". `attestConvergence({demaHome, operatorPubkeyPem, now})`runs the convergence verifier and, only if convergent, signs an attestation committing to the chain root,`chain_length`, `task_count`, a `task_fingerprint`(sha256 of the verified tasks), and the four`layers`. Pure-with-key-load (signs the verdict like SAT-VALIDATE-1A; no consent phrase, no disk write). `verifyConvergenceAttestation({attestation, demaHome, pubkeyPem})`is two-tier: **Level-A** re-derives`attestation_id`+ verifies the Ed25519 signature under the EXTERNAL pubkey; **Level-B** re-runs`verifyConvergentTaskChain` against the LIVE chain and confirms the attested verdict still re-derives (chain root, length, task fingerprint). Schema:`bizra.dema.convergence_attestation.v0.1`; rule:`flywheel_task_convergence.v0.1`. 6 tests. THE GROUNDING CATCH: tampering a bound entry AFTER attesting leaves the signature valid (`level_a_signature_valid:true`) but fails Level-B (`stage:"grounding", reason:"live_chain_not_convergent"`) — signed ≠ true, grounded = true. Fail-closed: a non-convergent chain cannot be attested (`not_convergent`); missing `now`→`created_at_iso_required`; local key ≠ `operatorPubkeyPem`→`operator_key_mismatch`; foreign-key verify → `signature_invalid`. Level-B grounding is TOTAL — every signed field re-derives from the live convergence result (`verifier_rule_id`, `convergence_schema`, `chain_length`, `canonical_chain_root`(from the verifier's single loaded snapshot),`task_count`, `layers`, `task_fingerprint`); a re-signed attestation with a wrong `task_count` passes Level-A but fails Level-B (`task_count_mismatch`). A malformed external pubkey fails closed (no crash) and a rotated local key (≠ `operatorPubkeyPem`) refuses to attest (`operator_key_mismatch`). No private-key/public-economy material. |

## Harness-only probes

| Probe                             | Surface covered                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| --------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `scripts/urp-stage4-closeout.mjs` | URP-4.1D Stage 4 Choose closeout drift-guard probe (`scripts/urp-stage4-closeout.mjs` registered in `scripts/check.mjs`): runs the real local proof/choose chain in a throwaway `mkdtemp` `DEMA_HOME` (`authorship key init` with `GENERATE AUTHORSHIP KEY` consent -> `authorship sign` with `SIGN AUTHORSHIP RECEIPT` consent -> `proof passport --json` -> `urp index --passport <p> --json` -> `urp verify <index> --json` -> `urp choose <index> --decision MARK_SHAREABLE --consent "MARK URP ENTRY SHAREABLE" --json` -> `urp choose <index> --decision MARK_LOCAL_ONLY --consent "MARK URP ENTRY LOCAL-ONLY" --json` -> `urp choose list --json` -> `urp choose verify <receipt> --json` for both generated choose receipts). Emits `bizra.dema.urp_stage4_closeout_demo.v0.1` with `demo_passed:true` and `truth_label:URP_STAGE_4_CHOOSE_CLOSEOUT_VERIFIED` on success. Failure emits `URP_STAGE_4_CHOOSE_CLOSEOUT_FAILED` and exits 1. Per-subprocess timeout: `URP_STAGE4_CLOSEOUT_TIMEOUT_MS` (default 30000). Cleans tmpdir in `finally`. No new CLI surface, no operator `DEMA_HOME` mutation, no network, no share publication, no PoI, no mint, no federation, no economic claim, and no persistent closeout receipt. |

## Quality expectations

Every new public surface should add or update tests for:

1. schema tag,
2. human output,
3. JSON output when available,
4. safe default,
5. non-execution boundary,
6. hostile or malformed input,
7. deterministic output when relevant.

## Coverage threshold

`npm run coverage` uses Node's native test coverage gate with enforced
thresholds:

```text
lines: 95
branches: 84
functions: 95
```

The primary GitHub Actions check and BIZRA Review Gate run this coverage command
after `npm test` and before `npm run check`.

## Secret-scanning gate (CI)

`.github/workflows/gitleaks.yml` runs gitleaks v8.30.1 on every pull request
and every push to main. Invariants:

- SHA-256-verified binary install (no marketplace-action license dependency)
- SHA-pinned `actions/checkout` reference (matches 100% SHA-pinning canon)
- `gitleaks detect --source . --no-banner --verbose --exit-code 1 --redact`
- Fails the workflow on any finding (`--exit-code 1`)
- Redacts secrets from CI logs (`--redact`) so the workflow output itself
  never becomes the leak surface
- Boundary: read-only audit · no repo write · no external publish

The gate is independent of `check.yml` and `bizra-review.yml` — a secret
finding fails its own workflow without blocking unrelated CI runs.
